The dictionary of English-Hokkienese dictionary in the Script of National Language of Swatow dialect

This dictionary is based on English-Chinese Vocabulary of the Vernacular Or Spoken Language of Swatow by Rudolf Lechler, Samuel Wells Williams, William Duffus
auther: Vòng Vân-Hạu
